Conclusion: 本方法能有效减小对完整3D物体模型的依赖，并在真实环境下实现稳健高效的双臂抓取，对实际机器人应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous grasping of large objects is a critical challenge in robotic manipulation. However, most existing studies focus on sequential manipulation rather than cooperative grasping, and methods addressing such bimanual tasks have largely been limited to simulation. These limitations stem from the difficulty of acquiring full 3D object models and generating physically plausible grasping actions. To fill this gap, we propose a real-world bimanual grasping framework that includes: a multimodal dataset capturing joint angles, visual observations and force signals; a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM)-based module that generates joint-level grasp configurations from segmented point clouds; and an execution strategy that integrates motion planning with online grasp refinement to ensure physical stability and feasibility. Our approach enables the synthesis of executable bimanual grasps from single-view inputs, reducing dependence on complete 3D object models and ensuring stable real-world performance. Experiments on a dual-arm robot demonstrate high success rates across unseen objects with varying geometries and poses, and ablation studies confirm the contributions of key components of our system.

</details>


### [183] [Hip Energized Monopedal Hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10387)
*Shane Rozen-Levy,Griffon McMahon,Daniel Koditschek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新型的平面单足机器人步态控制策略，实现了能量损失补偿与前进速度-高度的调节，理论分析及实验均验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PD+前馈控制方法在稳定机器人俯仰时会产生反作用力矩，导致因阻尼带来的能量耗散难以补偿，从而影响步态稳定和性能。为实现高效和可控的机器人行走，有必要设计更有效的能量管理和分配机制。

Method: 作者通过调整质心位置提高俯仰稳定力矩，将其作为补充步态能量的方式。提出新的步态策略，在径向与角向自由度间分配能量，同时用混合平均分析法得到步态的定点及特征值的封闭形式，使参数影响一目了然。该方法在仿真（包括5连杆模型和Penn Jerboa机器人的精准模型）和实际机器人实验中均得到测试和验证。

Result: 数学分析预测与仿真仿真及实际实验结果高度吻合。Penn Jerboa机器人在1.02 m/s到1.77 m/s（5.10~8.85腿长/秒）范围内实现了稳定步态和期望的能量分配。

Conclusion: 所提控制策略能有效补偿因阻尼导致的能量损失，实现步态的高度和速度调节，具有很强的理论指导和实际应用价值。

Abstract: We present a novel stepping strategy for pitch unlocked planar monopeds where the reaction torques from stabilizing pitch with a conventional PD + feedfoward controller are recruited to counteract energetic losses from damping. By moving the location of the mass center, our controller increases the pitch stabilization torque, thereby adding energy to the gait. A new stepping policy adjusts the distribution of energy between the radial and angular degrees of freedom to counteract dissipative losses and achieve a user specified balance between steady state fore-aft speed and apex height. Hybrid averaging analysis yields closed form expressions for the fixed points and eigenvalues of the resulting gait, lending insight into the interplay between the physical and control parameters' influence on performance. Simulation studies on a generic 5 link biped and a careful model of the Penn Jerboa reveal a useful correspondence to these analytical predictions. Physical experiments on the Penn Jerboa exhibit stable locomotion with speeds ranging from 1.02 m/s to 1.77 m/s (5.10 leg lengths/s to 8.85 leg lengths/s) in a manner effectively approximated by the mathematical analysis.

</details>


### [184] [PBD-AG: Persistent Baseline-Delta Active Graphs with Uncertainty-Aware Inspection for Long-Horizon Service Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10449)
*Shuo Bao,Wei Dong,Shuyue Zhang,Ming Shang,Yuchen Huang,Han Yu,Chengjie Xu,Yiheng Bi,Kai Sun,Fuchun Sun,Xinzhou Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向长期任务服务机器人，能够自动构建和持续修正环境模型的新框架PBD-AG，实现了对动态物体的高效、可靠追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前机器人的地图构建方法存在累积误差、无法捕捉持久物体变化、缺少三维几何证据等问题。为实现机器人在新环境下长期自主工作，需要更健壮和可更新的世界模型。

Method: 提出了PBD-AG框架，将环境中的恒定特征与动态事件解耦，机器人自主探索并构建基准结构，通过层次对象信念实现对物体状态的追踪。引入加权置信度，对物体几何、语义、身份等状态管理，并用可见性门控减少因遮挡导致的误删，采用图结构的策略优化机器人观察路径。

Result: 在多种环境和动态仿真评测下，PBD-AG在物体追踪精度、身份连续性和变动事件识别上优于对比方法。物理机器人实验证明了该方法的可集成性和长期追踪能力。

Conclusion: PBD-AG能够为长期服务机器人提供可持续、可追溯的世界模型，显著提升了动态场景下的环境感知与物体管理能力，具备良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Long-horizon service robots require persistent world models that can be built autonomously in unseen environments and revised as task-relevant objects change. Existing methods rely on online mapping, which accumulates localization and observation errors, static scene representations that cannot capture persistent object changes, or holistic vision-language predictions that lack verifiable 3D geometric evidence. We present PBD-AG, a persistent baseline-delta active graph framework that decouples robot-verified stable fixtures from revisable dynamic object events. Under our framework, the robot autonomously bootstraps the structural baseline from onboard exploration and inspects discovered fixtures to ground hierarchical object beliefs. PBD-AG maintains reliability-weighted object states over geometry, semantics, identity, existence, and support relations, utilizing a geometric visibility gate to mitigate false deletions under occlusion. Inspection viewpoints are selected by a graph-conditioned policy that balances target coverage, travel cost, collision risk, and redundant observation. Simulation experiments in multiple environments and under controlled dynamic evaluation show higher aggregate coarse-fixture F1 than capability-matched controls, as well as stronger identity continuity and event recall. A qualitative physical-robot demonstration further illustrates integration with onboard sensing, providing a traceable world model for long-horizon robotic perception.The project page of PBD-AG is available at https://shuobao214.github.io/PBD-AG/

</details>


### [185] [Lost in Reconstruction: Aligning Action Representations with Language in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10484)
*Li Wenjie,Yash Jangir,Ignacy Stepka,Yash Agarwal,Marion Kipsang,Yonatan Bisk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的动作标记方法SALT，从语义层面对动作进行离散化，提升了视觉-语言-动作模型中动作与语言的对应能力，从而在机器人控制任务中取得更好表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）中的动作表示通常只优化重建误差，这种做法在动作离散化时容易丢失动作与语言（例如动词）之间的本质联系，导致语义失真，从而制约基于语言指令的机器人控制效果。

Method: 作者分析了动作轨迹中蕴含的语义信息，指出仅用重建损失的离散化方案不足。为此提出SALT方法，它在基于矢量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE）的动作标记器基础上，额外引入辅助任务——要求冻结的视觉-语言模型能够从标记化后的动作表征中恢复出原始指令语句，从而实现动作与语言的语义对齐。

Result: 在SimperEnv环境中的实验表明，采用SALT训练的策略平均成功率达71.9%，远高于仅重建VQ-VAE标记器（42.7%）和其他方法（如FAST，31.2%）。SALT还能分化出与动词相对应的专门标记，同时保持动作重建的准确性。

Conclusion: 机器人动作轨迹本身包含丰富的语言锚定信息，若在动作表示中特意保留和强化这些结构，可以显著提升基于语言指令的控制性能。

Abstract: Action verbs describe not only the physical outcomes of actions, but also how those actions are performed. Yet action representations in vision-language-action models (VLAs) are typically optimized for reconstruction under L1/L2 losses in raw action space, where numerical proximity need not reflect linguistically meaningful distinctions. On BridgeV2, we show that action trajectories contain verb-grounding information beyond visual state changes, and that reconstruction-only discrete tokenization systematically erodes this information. To address this problem, we introduce SALT, a Semantically ALigned action Tokenizer that augments a VQ-VAE-style tokenizer with an auxiliary objective requiring a frozen vision-language model to recover the episode instruction from quantized action latents. Policies trained with SALT achieve 71.9% average success in SimplerEnv, compared with 42.7% for a reconstruction-only VQ-VAE tokenizer and 31.2% for FAST. SALT also develops verb-specialized codes while maintaining reconstruction fidelity. These results show that robot action trajectories provide a source of language grounding and that preserving this structure in action representations can substantially improve language-conditioned control.

</details>


### [186] [JitTrack: Onboard Multi-Object Tracking Against Viewpoint Jitter for Agile UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10485)
*Yachun Shan,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种名为JitTrack的多目标跟踪系统，专为应对无人机剧烈运动导致的视角抖动问题设计，实现了更稳定、鲁棒的多目标跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机多目标跟踪方法主要在离线环境下评估，缺乏对实际飞行中相机急剧运动和目标跟随需求的支持，特别是面对剧烈视角抖动时目标匹配困难、跟踪性能下降。该研究旨在提升无人机机载多目标跟踪在现实场景（尤其是运动剧烈时）的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了JitTrack框架，基于query-based transformer进行目标跟踪，创新点包括：（1）语义细化机制提升新目标的检测能力；（2）运动感知的查询纠正，有效补偿由视角抖动导致的目标错位；（3）运动启发的噪声训练策略，通过模拟相机运动提升模型鲁棒性。此外，集成了感知-规划-控制的闭环追踪流程，实现与无人机动力学自洽的主动目标跟随。

Result: 在公开无人机MOT基准上，JitTrack方法在性能上较基线有持续性的提升。同时，真实无人机飞行实验显示该系统在剧烈视角抖动情况下依然能够实现有效、实用的机载视觉跟踪。

Conclusion: JitTrack框架可显著提升无人机机载多目标跟踪应对剧烈相机运动时的稳健性，为智能无人机实现现实世界环境下的目标感知与自主跟随提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) onboard agile unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) remains challenging due to severe viewpoint jitter induced by camera ego-motion. Rapid attitude changes during flight often lead to significant target displacement across frames, causing inaccurate target association and degraded tracking performance. Existing UAV MOT methods are primarily evaluated on offline benchmarks and seldom address the practical requirements of real-world onboard deployment, including robustness to camera motion and active target following. To address these challenges, we propose JitTrack, an active onboard multi-object tracking framework that accommodates drone dynamics and camera ego-motion. Built upon a query-based transformer tracker, JitTrack introduces semantic refinement to improve the detection of emerging targets, motion-aware query rectification to compensate for target misalignment caused by viewpoint jitter, and a motion-inspired denoising training strategy that simulates camera motion patterns for robust supervision. Furthermore, we develop a perception-planning-control closed-loop tracking pipeline for real-world deployment, enabling collision-free and physically feasible target following on agile UAVs. Extensive experiments on public UAV MOT benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over the baseline method, while real-world flight experiments validate the effectiveness and practicality of JitTrack for robust onboard visual tracking under viewpoint jitter.

</details>


### [187] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control via Sequential Convex Programming for Drone-to-Drone Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10542)
*Neeraj Balachandar,Shriram Hari,Vishnu R. Unni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用序列凸规划（SCP）进行自主多旋翼飞行器空中对接的优化方法，在目标有扰动运动时，能实现高精度、鲁棒的对接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在不确定扰动（如风干扰、目标自身运动）和测量噪音情况下，实现无人机与运动目标的空中对接极为困难。传统方法在动态约束和对接精度上受限，因此有必要发展新颖的轨迹优化和鲁棒估计方法来提升对接可靠性与安全性。

Method: 将空中对接表述为含有扰动状态的有限时域最优控制问题，采用降阶非线性动力学建模，通过序列凸规划（SCP）在预测控制框架下在线求解最优轨迹，并融合带噪声的状态估计，实现对目标扰动运动的鲁棒预测。通过MuJoCo高保真仿真平台进行轨迹验证。

Result: 方法能在目标静止和匀速运动情况下，可靠收敛至对接接口，并严格满足几何约束（对接锥角低至10度时仍无明显违规），末端状态误差维持在容差以内。在有风扰动标准差达0.5的情况下，仍然保持稳定推进速度和受控努力，实现鲁棒的对接表现。

Conclusion: SCP轨迹优化框架能够在存在扰动与估计不确定性的情形下，实现鲁棒、精确的多旋翼空中对接，应用潜力强。

Abstract: Autonomous mid-air docking of multi-rotor vehicles under disturbance-driven target motion poses a constrained non-linear trajectory optimization challenge. This work formulates the docking task as a finite-horizon optimal control problem based on a reduced-order nonlinear model augmented with disturbance states. The resulting problem is solved using sequential convex programming within a receding-horizon framework to generate dynamically feasible docking trajectories. State estimation with noisy measurements is incorporated to enable robust relative motion prediction, while trajectory execution is validated in a high-fidelity rigid-body MuJoCo simulation environment. The proposed framework is evaluated for stationary and constant-velocity target motions, demonstrating reliable convergence to the docking interface while satisfying geometric capture constraints. Quantitatively, the method maintains negligible docking-cone violations and terminal state errors within prescribed tolerances, and achieves consistent, safe docking performance for cone half-angles as low as 10 degrees. Robust operation is observed for wind disturbance levels up to a standard deviation of 0.5, while preserving bounded approach velocities and stable control effort. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the SCP-based trajectory optimization framework for disturbance-robust aerial docking under estimation uncertainty.

</details>


### [188] [BooST: Bridging Semantics and Motions for Efficient Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10600)
*Jusuk Lee,Daesol Cho,Jonghun Shin,Seungyeon Yoo,Jonghae Park,Taekbeom Lee,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了BooST框架，实现了机器人技能抽象在泛化、鲁棒性和效率三方面的兼顾，通过统一语义意图和动态动作的技能表征，提升了机器人技能迁移和快速适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人技能迁移面临泛化、鲁棒性与高效性难以兼得的问题。已有方法往往只关注语义意图或运动动作其中之一，导致技能迁移不完整，下游任务需要大量数据适应。

Method: BooST是一种两阶段框架，第一阶段利用跨模态VQ-VAE同时捕获技能的语义意图和运动动态，得到统一表征；第二阶段将表征蒸馏至轻量级策略，方便下游任务高效适应。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人环境中的大量实验显示，BooST在少样本快速适应、跨领域技能迁移及动态视觉干扰鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法，同时拥有高效、易部署的模型设计。

Conclusion: BooST有效解决了机器人技能迁移的主要难点，能为实际机器人部署带来更强的适应性和泛化能力，是推动机器人能力提升的重要框架。

Abstract: Skill abstraction---the process of learning reusable and temporally extended behaviors---has emerged as a key paradigm for improving sample efficiency and generalization in robot learning. For efficient skill transfer to real robots, learned skills must generalize across tasks and domains, remain robust to visual and dynamic perturbations, and be efficient enough for practical deployment. However, existing methods typically satisfy only a subset of these properties, as they capture either high-level semantic intent (what) or low-level motion dynamics (how). This incomplete skill transfer yields weak priors for policy learning, thereby demanding substantial in-domain data for downstream adaptation. To address these challenges, we introduce BooST, a two-stage framework that explicitly bridges semantics and motions to satisfy all three desiderata. BooST first leverages a cross-modal VQ-VAE to capture both semantic intent and motion dynamics, yielding a unified skill representation. It then distills this representation into a lightweight policy for efficient downstream adaptation to new tasks. Extensive experiments across simulation and real-robot settings demonstrate that BooST achieves superior few-shot adaptation, cross-domain skill transfer, and robustness to dynamic visual distractors, while maintaining a lightweight yet expressive design suitable for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [189] [Toward the Cognitive--Physical Limits of Embodied Intelligence through a World-Model-Centric Autonomous Racing Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10618)
*Zitong Shan,Baichuan Lou,Yanxin Zhou,Shuge Wu,Xianqi He,Bolin Zhao,Sheng Zhao,Zhouheng Li,Chee Kiong Ong,King Ho Holden Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以世界模型为核心的自主赛车智能体，通过在极限条件下的真实数据采集和闭环优化，显著提升了智能体对自身能力极限的认知和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身智能系统普遍只在保守或中等环境下评测，对于极限条件下的认知和控制能力了解不足。自主赛车作为极限测试平台，能推动具身智能体在高动态、高风险下认知和行动能力的研究。

Method: 作者提出一种以世界模型为中心的自主赛车框架，通过从真实车辆在极限下的表现（包括成功和失败）中学习，建立可预测的世界模型，然后在此基础上进行未来驱动的决策与控制，并通过数据和策略的闭环优化不断提升系统对认知和物理边界的掌控。

Result: 实车测试中，智能体在最高时速256.3公里/小时和峰值横向加速度26.8米/秒²下，依然能保持稳健的本地化和感知能力。在全尺寸仿真赛道上，该智能体在各类高难度场景下实现88.3%的互动成功率。闭环优化过程中，智能体对极限的利用、故障恢复能力及在不同环境/新赛道下的泛化能力均有提升。

Conclusion: 世界模型驱动方法有助于具身智能体精准识别和推演自身能力边界，能够实现更安全、适应性更强的现实部署，为高风险任务下的智能体研究指明了新方向。

Abstract: Embodied artificial intelligence aims to develop agents that perceive, reason, and act through continuous interaction with the physical world. However, most embodied systems are still evaluated within conservative safety margins or moderate interaction regimes, leaving their capability boundaries under extreme conditions insufficiently understood. Autonomous racing provides a stringent testbed by combining high-frequency localization and perception, adversarial interaction, near-saturated vehicle dynamics, and strict safety constraints. Existing systems push high-speed performance but rarely model and refine cognitive and physical limits jointly. Here we show that a world-model-centric autonomous racing agent provides a concrete step toward exploring these coupled limits. The framework learns predictive world models from near-limit successes and failures to capture interaction evolution, ego dynamics, and feasible-motion boundaries, coupling world-state construction, future-aware reasoning, and near-limit control in a closed-loop refinement process. Training data were collected from real-vehicle autonomous racing, where the onboard system maintained robust localization and perception at speeds up to 256.3 km/h and peak lateral acceleration of 26.8 m/s$^2$. In full-scale simulated racing, the well trained world-model-centric agent achieves an 88.3% interaction success rate across various challenging simulated racing scenarios. Closed-loop refinement of the world model and policy further improved utilization of cognitive-physical limits, recovery from failure modes, and generalization across varying conditions and unseen circuits. These results suggest a boundary-aware methodology in which world models help embodied agents represent, predict, and continually refine their capability boundaries for safer real-world deployment.

</details>


### [190] [When Your State Estimator Has Lost The Plot: Detecting Estimator Failures Via Spectral Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10623)
*Christian Lanegger,Helen Oleynikova,Roland Siegwart,Michael Pantic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于频域分析的传感器无关自省方法，用于评估机器人状态估计器的健康状况，并在真实室外数据集上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人状态估计在遇到未建模干扰（如传感器混叠或异常噪音）时，容易失效。传统估计器的置信度度量往往过于乐观，且对数据分布敏感，缺乏普适、自省的健康评估机制。

Method: 方法基于对最近速度估计的频域功率分布进行分析。通过研究信号功率、频谱带宽、熵等频域指标，评估估计质变，并适用于不同传感器（视觉、激光雷达、雷达）组合的里程计。

Result: 在包含多种估计器失效场景的无人机室外飞行数据上，频域指标能检测出51%-58%的标注故障，且不同估计框架下精度为60%-84%。健康与退化估计在谱功率上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 简单的频域分析可作为轻量级的自省工具，为现有鲁棒性技术提供补充，有望拓展机器人估计器的应用和自诊断能力。

Abstract: Reliable onboard state estimation is essential for safe robotic operation, yet unmodeled disturbances, such as sensor aliasing or out-of-distribution noise, still cause estimators to degrade or fail completely. While many methods aim to improve estimator robustness, only a few provide introspective mechanisms to assess estimate quality. Existing uncertainty measures, such as covariances, rely on idealized assumptions and tend to be overconfident, and more recent data-driven approaches are typically tied to their training data distributions.
  We propose a sensor-agnostic introspective method that assesses estimator health by analyzing the frequency-domain power distribution of recent velocity estimates. The method is evaluated using outdoor flight data from an aerial robot running visual-inertial, LiDAR-inertial, and radar-inertial odometry. The dataset includes multiple estimator failures, enabling analysis of several frequency-domain indicators, such as signal power, spectral bandwidth, and entropy. We observe consistent spectral power differences between healthy and degraded estimates, allowing detection of 51%-58% of labeled failures with 60%-84% precision across three fundamentally different state estimation frameworks. Our results show that even a simple frequency-domain analysis of a state estimator's output can serve as a lightweight introspective tool to complement existing robustness techniques in real-world robotic deployments, and opens promising avenues for future investigation.

</details>


### [191] [OAA: Three Phases of Vocal Guidance in Human-Drone Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10651)
*Allan Henry,Christian Graff,Solange Rossato,José-Ernesto Gomez-Balderas,Sylvain Huet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文发现，在语音引导的远程操作任务中，无论是人与人之间还是人与无人机之间的协作，语音指导自然而然地分为三个运动和语言上区别明显的阶段：定向、接近和调整。这一三阶段结构可能是人类空间指导的内在特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语音的机器人控制系统通常将语音指令视为静态流，忽略了操作过程中文本和动作随任务进展而发生的变化。作者旨在研究并揭示语音引导的动态变化规律，以更好地支持自适应远程操作系统设计。

Method: 作者通过两种实验设置：1) 人与人之间通过手指指示进行空间引导（N=10组）；2) 人与无人机之间通过手柄控制进行远程操作（N=29组）。采集了运动捕捉与语音数据，并通过变点检测算法分析3D轨迹信号，结合统计方法（Kruskal-Wallis检验，p<.001）验证不同阶段的存在与特征。

Result: 无论是在人与人还是人与无人机的配置下，语音指导均自发形成三个阶段（定向、接近、调整），且这些阶段在运动轨迹和词汇使用上有明显差异；旋转类词汇出现在定向阶段，而接近阶段稀缺此类词汇，弱化类语词汇则在调整阶段积累。多种语言和非语言信号共同标记阶段切换，且静默也是重要信号。不同配置下均能复制该结构，说明其普适性。

Conclusion: 三阶段的语音指导结构是人类空间指导的内在属性，而不是实验条件的产物。该发现为开发具备阶段感知（OAA-aware）自适应语音控制系统提供理论依据，对提升远程语音引导系统的自然性与效率具有重要意义。

Abstract: Voice-guided teleoperation requires systems that adapt to the evolving dynamics of human guidance. Yet most voice-controlled robot systems treat spoken commands as a stationary stream, ignoring how the guide's communicative behavior changes as the task progresses. Using motion capture and speech data from two experimental configurations, humanhuman guidance (finger pointing, N =10 dyads) and humandrone teleoperation (gamepad control, N =29 dyads), we show that spontaneous vocal guidance consistently organizes into three kinematically and linguistically distinct phases: Orientation, Approach, and Adjustment. These phases are identified automatically via change point detection on 3D trajectory signals, and validated statistically (Kruskal-Wallis, p<.001). Three lexical families replicate across configurations: rotation vocabulary marks Orientation, translation vocabulary is scarce there, and attenuators accumulate toward Adjustment. Together with inter-utterance silence, these cues mark the Orientation boundary that speech rate alone leaves unmarked. The same three-phase structure emerges in both configurations despite radically different motor interfaces, suggesting it is an intrinsic property of human spatial guidance rather than an artifact of the experimental setup. We discuss implications for OAA-aware adaptive control in voice-guided teleoperation.

</details>


### [192] [Robust Sliding Mode and Admittance Control of Underactuated Aerial Manipulators for Contact-Based Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10656)
*Tareq Aziz Alqutami,Yvan Petillot,Matthew W. Dunnigan,Mustafa Suphi Erden*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于六旋翼无人机搭载单自由度机械臂进行接触式工业检测的强鲁棒控制框架，有效实现了高精度力控制与姿态稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触式工业检测对飞行平台的稳定性和抗扰能力有较高要求，但欠驱动飞行器（如六旋翼搭载机械臂）因动力学耦合带来控制难题，需要更高效强鲁棒控制方法。

Method: 整体采用滑模控制（SMC）结合积分补偿实现轨迹跟踪，同时利用响应控制律(admittance control)进行力调节。接触力转化为前馈姿态项，机械臂主动补偿飞行器姿态偏转，以维持与检测表面的对齐。通过软硬件在环仿真对策略进行验证并与PID方法对比。

Result: 利用SMC比传统PID方法具有更优的跟踪精度和耦合抑制能力；力控制RMSE为0.12N，系统可稳定输出最大20N接触力，满足工业检测需求。

Conclusion: 所提控制架构能实现稳定、可靠的接触式检测任务，提升了欠驱动飞行操控系统的力控性能和应用潜力。

Abstract: Contact-based industrial inspection requires aerial platforms to maintain stable interaction while rejecting disturbances. Underactuated aerial manipulators present control challenges due to the dynamic coupling between vehicle attitude and force generation. This paper proposes a robust control framework for an underactuated hexarotor equipped with a 1-DoF manipulator to perform sustained contact inspection. The architecture integrates integral-augmented Sliding Mode Control (SMC) for trajectory tracking with an admittance control law for force regulation. The contact force is mapped to a feedforward attitude term, while the 1-DoF arm actively compensates for the tilt to maintain surface alignment. Software-in-the-loop simulations demonstrate that the SMC-based approach achieves superior tracking and coupling rejection compared to traditional PID. Furthermore, the interaction strategy achieved precise force regulation with an RMSE of 0.12N and was able to stably exert up to 20N force, confirming the system's efficacy for stable, reliable contact-based inspection.

</details>


### [193] [TCAM for Autonomous Deformable Manipulation: The RMC2 Champion System for WBCD 2026 Track 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10718)
*Guangrui Shen,Zhili He,Shigang Wang,Yuanjun Sun,Qing Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RMC2团队在WBCD 2026变形操作挑战赛中取得冠军，提出了基于TCAM框架的全自主T恤抓取与铺平系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比赛要求机器人需全自动完成T恤从堆叠中分离、装载、对齐和抚平，涉及复杂的变形物体操作，对硬件、感知和算法均提出高要求，且比赛极大鼓励完全自治方案，激发作者设计高效自主解决流程。

Method: 系统以TCAM因果行为模型为核心，强调硬件、感知、数据与学习一体化协同。硬件上采用了定制3D打印夹爪提升单层分离可靠性，并基于双臂平台与四相机方案实现远近结合感知，结合UMI类演示及真实机器人演示实现泛化与特定性。TCAM分析操作失败物理原因以驱动有针对性再采集与策略微调，策略基于多视角VLA骨干网络输出动作序列。

Result: 系统在决赛中表现出色，平均每次大约23秒操作T恤，总共加载25件，有22次达到要求的抚平标准，获得挑战冠军。

Conclusion: 该方案展现了多模态感知与因果数据增补下提升变形物体操作自主性的有效性，为相关领域全自动变形操作提供了实际且可推广的技术路径。

Abstract: This technical report describes the RMC2 Team's champion solution for the WBCD 2026 Track 4: Deformable Manipulation Challenge. The task requires a robot to pick a single T-shirt from a stack, load it onto a printing pallet, align the collar with a target area, and smooth the printing region, a sequence that involves single-layer separation, deformable transport, precise placement, and contact-rich surface adjustment. The competition strongly incentivizes fully autonomous execution, motivating the development of an autonomous solution. We built a fully autonomous system around the TCAM (TermiBrain Causal Action Model) framework, with the design principle that hardware, perception, data, and learning should jointly reduce the physical interaction complexity the policy must handle. A custom 3D-printed gripper designed for single-layer fabric separation improves picking reliability on a dual-arm ARX X5 platform. A wrist-centric four-camera setup pairs upper fisheye cameras for task-level context with lower RGB cameras for close-range gripper-cloth contact observation. We combine portable UMI-style demonstrations with real-robot demonstrations collected on the deployable platform to provide both broad manipulation priors and deployment-specific dynamics. TCAM ties these components into a closed loop: each trajectory is analyzed to identify the physical factors contributing to its outcome, driving targeted data recollection and policy fine-tuning. The policy outputs 30-step end-effector delta-pose action chunks from a multi-view VLA backbone. In the final competition, our system loaded 25 T-shirts at an average of approximately 23 seconds per attempt, with 22 achieving the required surface smoothness, securing first place in Track 4.

</details>


### [194] [Embodied Multimodal Grounding for Open-Vocabulary Mobile Manipulation via Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10756)
*Huosen Ou,Dongni Song,Yuncong Wang,Tao Zhou,Yiding Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种结合3D语义感知和多模态策略的新框架，提升了家庭环境中机器人基于语言和视觉信息的目标定位与操作能力，在多项真实机器人实验中表现出显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视-语-动作（VLA）系统在物品识别、定位以及实际操作中，易因遮挡、杂乱和视角变化而失败，尤其是在家庭等真实环境中。作者希望提高机器人对开放词汇目标的指令理解及操控成功率，解决现有系统在复杂场景下的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 提出了一种以主动多视角语义3D高斯斑点（Semantic-3DGS）为核心的多模态语义对齐框架。方法包括：主动感知收集局部多视角数据，基于语言条件下实现3D定位、带可达性分析的底座调整，以及扩散式视觉-语言-动作策略。语义3D特征仅在决策模型后期嵌入，以保留已有动作先验。

Result: 在真实机器人50次实验中，相较于PointVLA和DexVLA分别获得60%、40%、28%的长任务成功率；在杂乱环境下多视角方案成功率74%，优于单视角方案（52%）及PointVLA（46%）；在75cm高度变换下仍保持75%成功率，并消除了视觉误抓现象。

Conclusion: 引入可动态刷新的3D语义对齐机制，能显著提升机器人在复杂、遮挡、多视角和体型限制等场景下的感知与操作鲁棒性，优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Embodied mobile manipulation requires language, visual observations, three-dimensional scene structure, and action feasibility to be aligned before execution. We study open-vocabulary target grounding with few-shot manipulation in local household workspaces and present an embodied multimodal grounding framework that integrates active multi-view Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (Semantic-3DGS), reachability-aware base positioning, and a diffusion-based vision-language-action policy. A task-driven local Semantic-3DGS serves as a shared interface across active sensing, language-conditioned 3D localization, obstacle-aware scene reasoning, base preparation, and semantic conditioning of the action model. To preserve pretrained action priors, the 3D semantic cues are injected only into the late action-expert blocks. In expanded 50-trial real-robot evaluations against representative vision-language-action (VLA) approaches, the full system achieves 60% long-horizon success compared with 40% for PointVLA and 28% for DexVLA, and reaches 74% success in heavily cluttered manipulation compared with 52% for the single-view variant and 46% for PointVLA. It also maintains 75% success under a 75 cm height shift and eliminates photo-induced false grasps. These results indicate that explicit, refreshable 3D semantic grounding can improve robustness under clutter, occlusion, viewpoint variation, and embodiment constraints.

</details>


### [195] [JEPA-WAM: Stage-Level Joint-Embedding Prediction for World-Action Models in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10780)
*Xiao Liu,Yuguang Yang,Xi Wang,Kai Jiang,Cheng Chi,Yong Xu,Wenchao Ding,Yilun Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法JEPA-WAM，通过结合短期物理未来和阶段级语义未来，提升了通用机器人操作策略的泛化与效率，在50项任务中实现了高成功率和更快执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用机器人策略通常只预测短期未来（如几秒的视频动作片段），虽能应对具体动作执行，但无法明确描述任务从当前阶段进展到下一阶段的“阶段级”目标。提升机器人对任务阶段性理解，有助于更高效完成复杂任务。

Method: 作者提出JEPA-WAM方法。它以Motus的World Action Model (WAM)为基础，加入Stage-JEPA模块，通过冻结的V-JEPA2编码器获取当前状态表示，预测下一个推断阶段的潜在目标。方法融合了短期物理未来（局部场景变换）与阶段级语义未来（任务推进），并根据任务指令和观测动态推理下一阶段目标。

Result: JEPA-WAM在RoboTwin 2.0平台的50项操作任务测试中（包括标准与随机环境），总体完成率达90.25%，且在成功回合中，平均执行步数比最强基线方法减少5.97%。

Conclusion: 通过同时建模局部短期与阶段演进两种未来，JEPA-WAM显著提升了通用机器人在多任务、多环境下的成功率和执行效率，展示了结合物理和语义未来预测的重要性。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies aim to map multimodal observations and linguistic task instructions to actions across diverse tasks. However, existing methods typically represent the future as a fixed, short video-action chunk. This short-term future captures local scene evolution for action execution, but it does not explicitly describe the stage-level future that specifies how a task should progress from its current stage to the next. We therefore distinguish two complementary futures for robot manipulation: a short-term physical future to capture local scene evolution and a stage-level semantic future to represent task progress. We introduce JEPA-WAM, which augments a Motus-based World Action Model (WAM) with Stage-JEPA, a goal-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) predictor. Given the current observation and task instruction, Stage-JEPA uses a frozen V-JEPA2 encoder to extract the current-state representation and predicts the latent target of the next inferred stage. Across 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks in clean and randomized environments, JEPA-WAM achieves 90.25% overall success and reduces the mean number of execution steps in successful rollouts by 5.97% relative to the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [196] [Dual Stress: Runtime Safety Monitoring for Safety-Constrained MPC Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10791)
*Jamil Chahine,Wenqi Cai,John Abanes,Anthony Tzes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将模型预测控制器中的KKT乘子（dual stress signal）作为补充的碰撞危险监控信号，发现其能显著提高自动驾驶避险预警能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前自动导航中的危险监控主要依赖于几何信息（如预测清除距离、碰撞时间等），但忽视了模型预测控制中可以获得的KKT乘子信息，作者希望验证这些乘子是否能带来额外的安全监控价值。

Method: 作者提出将模型预测控制优化问题中的KKT乘子的加权和（dual stress signal）作为新的危险信号，并在物理模拟器中，通过固定场景和控制15种几何检测器进行对比实验，评估dual stress信号的有效性。

Result: dual stress信号能标记4.7倍于几何检测器未检测到但发生的碰撞数量（85比18）；两者结合时，能够提前预警3/4可避免碰撞，而单独用几何检测器只能预警不足一半。

Conclusion: KKT乘子（dual stress信号）作为危险监控补充，显著提升了自动驾驶安全预警能力，结合使用效果最佳。

Abstract: Runtime hazard monitors for autonomous naviga- tion are conventionally built from geometric quantities: predicted clearance, time to collision, and required deceleration. A model-predictive controller that enforces safety through explicit con- straints computes, as a by-product of every control step, a second information channel that such monitors ignore: the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker multipliers of its constrained optimization, which measure the marginal control effort spent to maintain safety against each obstacle. This paper evaluates whether a horizon-weighted sum of those multipliers, a dual stress signal, provides a hazard monitor complementary to the geometric warnings the same state already supports. We compare it against a battery of fifteen geometric detectors tuned to a matched false-alarm budget, on preregistered held-out crossing scenarios driven through a physics simulator. The stress alarm actionably flags 4.7 times as many collisions missed by the entire geometric battery as the geometric battery flags in return (85 versus 18); combined, the two channels warn of three quarters of the collisions for which braking remained feasible, against under half for the geometric battery alone.

</details>


### [197] [AECNav: Active Evidence Consolidation for Efficient Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10817)
*Guanlin Liu,Shaobin Ling,Renyuan Liu,Zeying Gong,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种训练无关、基于证据的目标导航新方法AECNav，在无先验训练和开放词汇场景下，能够高效准确地引导机器人找到指定目标，达到最新最高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本目标导航（ZSON）在无需任务特定训练的前提下，机器人需在未知环境中定位任意指定物体，但常面临感知冗余和目标确认依据不足，导致速度慢和准确率有限，因此亟需创新的感知-决策融合机制。

Method: 作者将ZSON视为证据驱动的感知到决策（perception-to-decision）问题，提出无训练的AECNav方法，包括：1）证据门控感知，复用统一语义编码消除冗余计算；2）证据整合，将检测结果聚合为合理的置信度分层，区别真实目标与伪目标，同时利用“未观测到”作为负面证据；3）主动证据获取，通过信息增益与行驶成本权衡，引导机器人在弱语义线索下有效探测。

Result: 在HM3D-v2、HM3D-OVON、MP3D等公开数据集上，AECNav分别取得84.7%、57.3%、51.3%的业界最好成功率，远优于现有方法，同时延迟（推理开销）显著降低。在实际机器人（四足机器人）测试中，40次实验达95％成功率，运行频率接近5Hz。

Conclusion: 提出的AECNav方法，无需任务特定训练，仅通过高效的证据整合机制，在开放词汇与未知环境下实现了高效率高准确的目标导航，为ZSON问题提供了新的解决思路并实现了业界领先性能。

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation (ZSON) in open-vocabulary scenarios is challenging, as it requires a robot to locate an arbitrarily specified object in an unseen environment without task-specific training. Currently, the task still suffers from high latency and limited accuracy due to redundant perception pipelines and insufficient evidence for reliable target confirmation. In this letter, we reframe ZSON as an evidence-driven perception-to-decision problem and present AECNav, a training-free pipeline built on three components: i) Evidence-gated perception, which utilizes a shared encoding across all reasoning stages to establish a unified semantic basis and eliminate redundant computations; ii) Evidence consolidation, which aggregates detections into cluster-level log-odds beliefs. This explicitly separates genuine target support from the false confidence of visually similar distractors, while treating the absence of expected detections as negative evidence; and iii) Active evidence acquisition, which sustains productive exploration under weak semantic cues by selecting frontiers that maximize information gain at minimal traversal cost. As a result, AECNav significantly outperforms previous methods and achieves state-of-the-art success rates of 84.7%, 57.3%, and 51.3% on HM3D-v2, HM3D-OVON, and MP3D, respectively, with substantially lower inference overhead, and attains 95% success across 40 trials on a physical quadruped robot at roughly 5Hz. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [198] [Neural Introspection Gating for Adaptive KV-Cache Reuse in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10824)
*Zhijie Wu,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进型缓存机制（Gated VLA-Cache），通过引入模型不确定性信号提升视觉语言动作模型的实时控制效率与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在实时推理时大量计算资源浪费在对不变视觉信息的重复编码，而已有缓存策略（如VLA-Cache）仅依赖视觉相似性判断，未考虑模型的不确定性，容易在一些情况下损失准确率。

Method: 提出Gated VLA-Cache方法，在视觉缓存基础上，引入一个基于输出动作token间logit margin的置信度门控机制。当检测到模型决策不确定（即logit margin低于阈值），则触发缓存失效并重新计算，从而动态平衡计算效率和准确率。该方法无需额外训练，零成本实现。

Result: 在LIBERO基准套件下，Gated VLA-Cache在OpenVLA与OpenVLA-OFT两种模型上实验，结果显示当盲目缓存降低性能时，新方法能够恢复100%以上的准确率损失，同时保留80%的计算节省。

Conclusion: Gated VLA-Cache显著提升了VLA模型的可靠性和计算效率，为视觉-语言-动作一体化控制提供了更优的实时推理解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action(VLA) models map camera images and language instructions directly to motor commands through a single autoregressive transformer. In real-time control, they still spend substantial compute recomputing key-value(KV) representations for visual tokens that barely change across neighboring frames. Recent work such as VLA-Cache reduces that cost by reusing KV states for visually static patches, but its policy relies only on observation-space heuristics and does not account for the model's own uncertainty. We propose Gated VLA-Cache, a lightweight, training-free extension that augments visual-similarity caching with neural introspection. The method monitors the logit margin between the top two predicted action tokens, a zero-cost confidence signal available during decoding. When the margin drops below a threshold, the cache is invalidated and a full recompute is triggered. Evaluated on four LIBERO benchmark suites with both OpenVLA and OpenVLA-OFT, Gated VLA-Cache improves reliability when blind caching hurts. On LIBERO-Goal and LIBERO-Long, it recovers over 100% of the lost accuracy while retaining 80% of the compute savings.

</details>


### [199] [Enabling Scalable Kinesthetic Teaching via Observer-based Hand-guiding with Active Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10847)
*Anna Tuma,Giuseppe Monetti,Jochen J. Steil,Niels Dehio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种名为RHOAS的全新机器人手引导方案，通过模型驱动的力估算技术，无需额外硬件即可主动辅助操作者运动，显著减小操作疲劳并提升教学演示质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前工业机器人手引导大多无主动辅助，长时间操作易导致人类操作者疲劳，影响模仿学习和教学质量。此外，其他多依赖昂贵传感器或任务相关先验知识，限制了在新任务下的适用性。

Method: 提出RHOAS手引导方案：将人机交互视为主动控制过程，通过模型外力估算、内部关节扭矩感知和冗余运动学，主动支持操作者的物理输入，克服传统基于被动顺应的手引导方法在人机主动交互时稳定性和实用性不足的问题。详细解决了因力估算器带来的关节弹性、不精确及重力补偿误差等挑战。

Result: 在KUKA LWR iiwa平台上，16名用户参与的实验显示：RHOAS方案下人机物理操作所需努力显著减少，复杂与敏捷任务的操作性均获得提升，用户总体偏好明确。

Conclusion: RHOAS在不增加硬件成本的前提下，实现对操作者意图的主动辅助，提高演示动作的表现能力，为机器人模仿学习和编程提供更优人机交互方式。

Abstract: Kinesthetic teaching through robot hand-guiding provides a natural interface for collecting demonstrations in imitation learning and programming-by-demonstration. However, extended sessions cause operator fatigue, reducing demonstration quality and limiting scalability. Current industrial hand-guiding approaches typically provide no active assistance, and alternatives require costly wrist-mounted force-torque sensors or rely on learned motion priors unavailable for new tasks. We propose RHOAS, a hand-guiding scheme that actively supports operator-intended motions using model-based force estimation without additional hardware. Our approach considers robot hand-guiding as an actively controlled interaction by the human operator, rather than an interaction with a passive environment. Standard methods used for hand-guiding typically rely on general passivity-based compliant control architectures that unnecessarily increase operator effort and limit the range of demonstrable motions without providing the intended stability guarantees in active interaction. Instead, our design utilizes model-based external torque estimation, internal joint torque sensing, and redundant robot kinematics to actively support human physical input within the human interaction frequency bandwidth. We address practical challenges of relying on observer-based force estimation, including suppression of unmodeled joint elastic dynamic effects and measurement noise in the feedback path, reduced estimate accuracy close to kinematic singularities, and static gravity compensation errors. In a user study with 16 participants on a KUKA LWR iiwa we demonstrate statistically significant reductions in physical effort, improved maneuverability for both precise and agile tasks, and clear user preference.

</details>


### [200] [Flex-$π$: A Multi-Stream World-Action Model with Compute Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10860)
*Ge Yan,Jinghao Liu,Yuzhi Fan,Lei Cai,Minwen Liao,Jesse Zhang,Dieter Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的世界-动作模型（WAM），可以无缝地将RGB、三维点云和对象语义等多种视觉信号整合到同一潜在空间中，共同用于机器人操作任务，实现高效泛化与演示效率，在多手抓取等任务上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM主要关注基于像素的RGB预测，缺乏对三维几何及对象语义的直接表征能力，限制了模型在复杂机器人操作等场景下的泛化与操作效率。

Method: 利用冻结的视频生成VAE，将RGB与3D点云映射到共享潜在空间，无须对点云特定训练。提出Flex-π模型（6B参数），在RGB、3D几何和对象语义多个信号间联合监督，并通过Mixture-of-Transformers骨干与流级dropout方式实现多模态输入灵活切换，支持各种输入子集，保持推理效率。

Result: 模型在复杂的现实双手操作任务上，展示出极强的演示效率和泛化能力，对比最强基线提升2-7倍，且推理速度优异。

Conclusion: 通过将不同视觉信号纳入同一潜在空间并高效融合，Flex-π在机器人多模态操作任务上实现了显著领先表现，为基于世界模型的泛化智能奠定了基础。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) predict the future to act better, but nearly all of them predict only RGB latents, trained purely for pixel reconstruction, with no explicit signal for the 3D geometry or object semantics manipulation needs. We find a surprising free lunch: the same frozen video-generation VAE that encodes RGB also encodes 3D pointmaps almost losslessly, with no pointmap-specific training at all. This lets us supervise Flex-$π$, a 6B-parameter WAM, on 3D geometry and object-centric DINO semantics alongside RGB, at no cost in new sensors, new pre-training, or inference latency. Every visual signal is projected into this shared latent space and denoised jointly with actions inside a Mixture-of-Transformers backbone; per-stream dropout with cross-modality forcing then lets a single trained checkpoint run on any subset of these streams, from a fast action-only mode to full joint generation. The result is a policy that is exceptionally demonstration-efficient and generalizes well, beating the strongest baselines by up to 2-7$\times$ on dexterous, precise, real-world bimanual manipulation tasks both in and out of distribution, all while running faster than $π_{0.5}$. Our project website: https://flex-pi.github.io/

</details>


### [201] [Aerial Layouting: Design and Control of a Compliant and Actuated End-Effector for Precise In-flight Marking on Ceilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10987)
*Christian Lanegger,Marco Ruggia,Marco Tognon,Lionel Ott,Roland Siegwart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种新型空中机器人系统，通过搭载基于Gough-Stewart机构的智能末端执行器，实现了空中机器人在毫米级精度下的接触性操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中机器人虽然能完成高精度飞行任务，如穿越狭窄开口和编队表演，但在需要更高定位精度（如毫米级）的接触性任务（如建筑现场布线、推滑等）上仍存在挑战。

Method: 设计了一种结合顺应性、多接触点、驱动性和自包含特性的新型智能末端执行器，并集成于空中机器人上。通过采用稳定性优化的Gough-Stewart机构提升空气中机器人接触操作的定位精度。

Result: 实验证明，所设计的智能末端执行器能实现所需的毫米级精度，并且能够稳定地在天花板上划线，无需对空中机器人进行精确建模或复杂控制。

Conclusion: 提出的系统和结构为空中机器人在高精度接触性操作任务（尤其适用于实际场景）提供了有效的解决方案，实验验证了该设计的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Aerial robots have demonstrated impressive feats of precise control, such as dynamic flight through openings or highly complex choreographies. Despite the accuracy needed for these tasks, there are problems that require levels of precision that are challenging to achieve today. One such problem is aerial interaction. Advances in aerial robot design and control have made such contact-based tasks possible and opened up research into challenging real-world tasks, including contact-based inspection. However, while centimetre accuracy is sufficient and achievable for inspection tasks, the positioning accuracy needed for other problems, such as layouting on construction sites or general push-and-slide tasks, is millimetres. To achieve such a high precision, we propose a new aerial system composed of an aerial vehicle equipped with a novel "smart" end-effector leveraging a stability-optimized Gough-Stewart mechanism. We present its design process and features incorporating the principles of compliance, multiple contact points, actuation, and self-containment. In experiments, we verify that the design choices made for our novel end-effector are necessary to obtain the desired positioning precision. Furthermore, we demonstrate that we can reliably mark lines on ceilings with millimetre accuracy without the need for precise modeling or sophisticated control of the aerial robot.

</details>


### [202] [Seeing above the waves: A modular sensing framework for data acquisition at sea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10997)
*Jonathan E. Schmidt,Julius Wirbel,P. Nicholas Hansen,Morgan Louédec,Christian L. H. Westerdahl,Dimitrios Dagdilelis,Roberto Galeazzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向海上自主船舶的模块化、可复现的传感平台设计蓝图，以提升感知系统的环境感知与船体自身状态感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋环境下传感器部署受船体结构和环境条件等限制，同时环境难以复现，数据采集困难，影响了自主船舶的研究与发展。亟需系统化、可复用的传感平台设计以推动研究标准化。

Method: 作者设计了一套集成多种传感器（雷达、激光雷达、IMU、GNSS、AIS、可见光与长波红外相机、气象传感器）的硬件平台，并配套基于ROS2的数据管理软件框架，实现模块化、长时间数据采集及硬件在环测试，支持现有传感器/算法集成。

Result: 该平台实现了硬件设计与数据采集方法的统一，提高了数据采集与实验的可复现性与可比性，能够为不同行业和科研项目提供通用标准，支持长期任务和复杂环境下的实验。

Conclusion: 该平台为推进海事自主导航及局势感知领域提供了标准化、可验证的数据基础，且为工程实现与科学研究间架起桥梁，有助于行业和学术界在自主船舶领域的持续发展和创新。

Abstract: Advancing autonomy for surface vessels requires systematic evaluation of their sensing and perception subsystems. Yet, maritime environments impose unique challenges: sensor installation is constrained by vessel layout, environmental conditions such as fog or sea clutter are difficult to reproduce, and long-duration missions complicate data collection. This work addresses the question: How can we design a modular and reproducible sensor platform for maritime autonomy?
  We present a comprehensive design blueprint that incorporates diverse modalities - RADAR, LiDAR, IMU, GNSS, AIS, RGB and LWIR cameras, and weather sensors - to enhance environmental awareness and vessel proprioception. Supported by a dedicated ROS2-based software framework for data management, our modular platform enables long-term data collection, hardware-in-the-loop testing, and integration with existing sensors and algorithms. By unifying hardware design and data capture methodology, the platform enhances reproducibility and comparability across vessels and research projects. The proposed framework bridges engineering implementation and research methodology, providing the foundation for standardized, verifiable datasets essential to advancing situational awareness and autonomous maritime navigation.

</details>


### [203] [Deployment Is Not Destiny: Robot Recomposition in the Field with Unseen Software, Hardware, and Compute Payloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11063)
*Steven Swanbeck,Jonathan Salfity,Jeffery Gunawan,Corrie Van Sice,Mitch Pryor,Robert Blake Anderson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种机器人在运行时可重组框架，实现对新硬件、软件和算力模块的即插即用整合，大幅缩短重构时间并提升适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数机器人各子系统耦合紧密，导致架构单一、难以在部署后适应新需求。传统手动集成需要专家，且耗时冗长，因此急需一种灵活、便捷的机器人扩展与适应方法。

Method: 作者设计了一个支持运行时重组的框架和抽象，允许用户快速集成未见过的硬件、软件模块和分布式算力，实现真正的即插即用；资源可在多台机器人分布式共享，无需开发者介入。

Result: 该方法将重配置时间降至几分钟，在两个灾害应急场景（核电厂放射性源定位、热成像搜寻被困者）进行了实地验证，机器人展现出快速重组及适应复杂任务的能力。

Conclusion: 提出的重组框架极大提升了机器人的灵活适应性与可扩展性，是机器人能随任务、技术和环境快速进化的重要一步。

Abstract: The tight coupling of subsystems in most robots, though a natural consequence of their complexity, leads to monolithic designs that are time-consuming and difficult to adapt after initial deployment. To address this challenge, we present a framework and supporting abstractions for recomposition during runtime that enable robots to quickly integrate previously unseen modular software, hardware, and compute payloads. Our approach allows non-expert users to quickly add new capabilities in the field through a true plug-and-play process. Crucially, new resources are not only immediately available to a host robot but are also shared with distributed peers, enabling compute-constrained systems to access powerful new remote capabilities. Our framework reduces reconfiguration time to a matter of minutes with no developer intervention, in stark contrast to the hours of expert effort often required for traditional manual integration. We demonstrate our method in two disaster response scenarios, including radioactive source localization at an operational nuclear reactor facility and a thermal-guided search for people in dark, difficult-to-reach spaces. These demonstrations show how in-field recomposition provides timely, flexible, and accessible adaptation to dynamic requirements, representing a critical step toward creating robots that can quickly evolve alongside the tasks, technologies, and environments they support.

</details>


### [204] [VIScore: Diagnosing Planning-Relevant Quality in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11174)
*Haiyu Wu,Randall Balestriero,Morgan Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了VIScore指标，通过对比两种不同的潜在空间正则化方法（SIGReg和VISReg），发现VISReg在提升世界模型规划成功率方面更有效，并且VIScore比以往指标更能解释规划成功的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然将潜在空间正则化为各向同性高斯分布有助于信息最大化和规划稳定性，但目前潜在空间质量和规划成功率之间缺乏直接联系。本文旨在理解并量化这一关系，为未来世界模型的设计与诊断提供依据。

Method: 研究中比较了SIGReg和VISReg两种正则化方法，探讨不同正则化属性及分布逼近精度对规划成功的影响。进一步提出综合评估编码器、预测器和规划器信息表现的新指标VIScore，并用Spearman相关性等方法与其他指标进行系统对比。

Result: 结果显示，VISReg优于SIGReg，能显著提升模型在OOD数据集上的规划成功率。VIScore在跨模型、跨任务和跨数据集上均获得超过0.75的Spearman相关性，准确反映了规划成功率，并且校准误差优于其它指标。

Conclusion: VISscore有效地量化并解释了规划成功的关键因素，是目前业界最优的预测规划成功率的评估工具。该指标为后续相关研究提供了统一、科学的测量方法，能促进世界模型的优化和诊断。

Abstract: Regulating the latent space to an isotropic Gaussian distribution provides a stable and information-maximized landscape for world model planning. However, the latent space property and successful planning remain disconnected. We first study this by comparing SIGReg and VISReg, two regularization loss functions with the same distribution target but different properties. Compared with SIGReg, VISReg has more flexibility in controlling the weights of center, scale, and shape regularization, and a larger batch size brings a finer distribution approximation. We find that the former, despite being beneficial in self-supervised learning (SSL), does not help the planning, whereas the latter improves the planning success on out-of-domain (OOD) datasets. This motivates a deep understanding of the factors that correlate with the success rate. Unlike the previous metrics focusing on the encoded latent only, we propose the Veracity-Influence-Sobriety score (VIScore), a metric that quantifies the reachability and capacity of a predictor given the encoded feature, and the hallucination of the searching-based planner. Compared with straightness, physical-state probing, and empowerment, we show that, with the measurement covering encoder, predictor, and planner, VIScore explains the success rate better than the others, as reflected by a strong Spearman correlation. Specifically, VIScore consistently achieves a Spearman correlation over 0.75 on both seen and unseen models and datasets on the cross-task success rate pool. Moreover, VIScore is the only metric that has a calibration error below the constant fit across all testing scenarios, showcasing the importance of these three aspects in planning success. We hope this metric can help future studies on world model design and diagnosis.

</details>


### [205] [Risk-Aware Kinodynamic Motion Planning Under Uncertainty For Safe Navigation on Planetary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11175)
*Sachin Sunil Kelkar,Tanmay Dokania,Yashwanth Kumar Nakka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合采样规划与非线性优化的风险感知运动规划方法，通过CVaR风险度量，显著降低了太空机器人运动的不确定性与风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主太空探索中的机器人需要在环境交互未知的情况下进行运动规划。环境和感知带来的不确定性会导致风险增加甚至任务失败。因此迫切需要兼顾成本与风险的运动规划方案。

Method: 采用两步法：第一，基于采样的AO-RRT（风险感知的快速随机树）算法生成动态可行、风险感知且渐近代价最优的轨迹；第二，将运动规划表述为非线性优化问题，并用序贯凸规划（SCP）进一步优化，以AO-RRT轨迹为初始值。风险以CVaR方式量化。

Result: 在仿真和实际硬件实验中，所提出方法能在运动轨迹上将风险降低约97%。

Conclusion: 本文方法有效解决了带不确定性的运动规划问题，显著降低了风险，证明了在实际太空机器人应用中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: For autonomous space exploration, robotic agents need to perform motion planning in which environmental interactions may be unknown. Learning these interactions, such as terrain mechanics for wheeled robots, can introduce uncertainties that lead to risky motion plans and potentially hazardous operations or mission failures. Moreover, uncertainties induced by perception-based systems can exacerbate the problem of safe motion planning. In this letter, we address the problem of performing cost-optimal kinodynamic motion planning with risk awareness. We approach this in two steps. First, a sampling-based planner (AO-RRT) generates a dynamically feasible, risk-aware, and asymptotically cost-optimal trajectory. Second, we formulate motion planning as a nonlinear optimization problem and solve it using sequential convex programming (SCP), using the AO-RRT trajectory as an initial solution. By quantifying risk using conditional value-at-risk (CVaR), we demonstrate a reduction in risk by over $\sim$97\% across trajectories in simulation and hardware experiments.

</details>


### [206] [Surgical WAM: A World-Action Model for Data-Efficient Surgical Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11204)
*Wenrui Bao,Tianyun Jiang,Zhiben Chen,Ser-Nam Lim,Peter D. Peng,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在手术机器人操作中利用无需动作标签的大量内窥镜视频预训练世界模型的方法，有效提升了在动作标签示例数量受限条件下的手术操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量、有动作标签的手术机器人演示数据十分稀缺且昂贵；纯视频数据则丰富廉价，但如何有效利用其帮助学习闭环手术操作策略仍未解决。

Method: 提出了Surgical World-Action Model（Surgical WAM），该模型首先利用无需动作标签的内窥镜视频学习外部世界视觉动态，再在有限动作标签数据上微调。最终模型可在部署时以闭环方式执行策略，并不断观察、 replanning。

Result: 在四个模拟手术操作任务中，采用视频预训练的闭环控制平均成功率从63.5%提升到77.8%，尤其在高接触和双手协作任务中提升显著。

Conclusion: 无需动作标签的视频为手术机器人控制策略提供了良好视觉动态先验，视频预训练是一种高效的数据利用路径，有助于推动手术机器人学习的规模和实用性。

Abstract: Learning reliable surgical manipulation policies is bottlenecked by the scarcity of action-labeled demonstrations: teleoperated surgical robot (e.g., dVRK) trajectories with synchronized kinematics are costly to collect, while surgical tasks demand precise contact handling, long-horizon reasoning, and bimanual coordination. Endoscopic video is comparatively inexpensive and abundant relative to synchronized video--kinematics trajectories, and a natural way to exploit it is to learn world models of surgical scenes. However, existing surgical world models use video primarily for simulation or policy evaluation, and rarely translate the learned dynamics into closed-loop control. This gap raises our central question: under a fixed budget of action-labeled demonstrations, does action-free video pretraining improve closed-loop surgical manipulation? To answer it, we introduce the Surgical World-Action Model (Surgical WAM), a unified generative model built on Cosmos Policy that jointly predicts future endoscopic observations and executable surgical robot action chunks. Surgical WAM first learns surgical visual dynamics from action-free video and is then fine-tuned on the fixed action-labeled budget; at deployment, it acts as a closed-loop, receding-horizon controller that executes a short prefix of each predicted action chunk and replans from the resulting observation. On a suite of four simulated surgical manipulation tasks, video pretraining improves the average success rate from 63.5% to 77.8%, including an absolute gain of 20 percentage points on PegTransfer, with the largest improvements on contact-rich and bimanual tasks. These results demonstrate that action-free video provides transferable visual dynamics priors for learning surgical robot control with limited action supervision, positioning data-efficient video pretraining as a practical path toward scaling up surgical robot learning.

</details>
